   record: dict[str, Any] = {\\\"path\\\": relative, \\\"type\\\": kind}\\n 165→            if kind == \\\"file\\\":\\n 166→                record[\\\"sha256\\\"] = file_digest(child)\\n 167→            elif kind == \\\"symlink\\\":\\n 168→                record[\\\"sha256\\\"] = hashlib.sha256(os.readlink(child).encode()).hexdigest()\\n 169→            else:\\n 170→ hashlib.sha256(os.readlink(path).encode()).hexdigest()\\n 185→    if kind == \\\"directory\\\":\\n 186→        return _directory_digest(path)\\n 187→    return _metadata_digest(path, observed)\\n 188→\\n 189→\\n 190→def _observe_entry(entry: dict[str, Any], roots: tuple[Path, ...]) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 191→    path = _absolute_clean_path(entry[\\\"path\\\"], f\\\"{entry['entry_id']} path\\\")\\n 192→    root = _containing_root(path, roots)\\n 193→    _reject_symlink_ancestors(path, root)\\n 194→    base = {\\n 195→        \\\"entry_id\\\": entry[\\\"entry_id\\\"],\\n 196→        \\\"kind\\\": entry[\\\"kind\\\"],\\n 197→        \\\"path\\\": str(path),\\n 198→        \\\"approved_root\\\": str(root),\\n 199→        \\\"action\\\": entry[\\\"action\\\"],\\n 200→        \\\"hash_mode\\\": entry[\\\"hash_mode\\\"],\\n 201→    }\\n 202→    try:\\n 203→        observed = path.lstat()\\n 204→    except FileNotFoundError:\\n 205→        return {\\n 206→           \\\"node_type\\\": \\\"absent\\\",\\n 212→            \\\"sha256\\\": None,\\n 213→        }\\n 214→    digest = (\\n 215→        _content_digest(path, observed)\\n 216→        if     \\\"realpath\\\": os.path.realpath(path),\\n 223→        \\\"device\\\": observed.st_dev,\\n 224→        \\\"inode\\\": observed.st_ino,\\n 225→        \\\"node_type\\\": _node_type(observed.st_mode),\\n 226→        \\\"sha256\\\": digest,\\n 227→    }\\n 228→\\n 229→\\n 230→def _parse_registry(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[tuple[Path, ...], list[dict[str, Any]]]:\\n 231→    _require_keys(payload, {\\\"schema_version\\\", \\\"approved_roots\\\", \\\"entries\\\"}, \\\"registry\\\")\\n 232→    if payload[\\\"schema_version\\\"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:\\n 233→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry schema_version 必须为 1.0\\\")\\n 234→    raw_roots = payload[\\\"approved_roots\\\"]\\n 235→    raw_entries = payload[\\\"entries\\\"]\\n 236→    if not isinstance(raw_roots, list) or not raw_roots:\\n 237→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry approved_roots 必须是非空列表\\\")\\n 238→    roots = tuple(_absolute_clean_path(value, \\\"approved_root\\\") for raw_entries:\\n 242→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry entries 必须是非空列表\\\")\\n 243→    entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\\n 244→    seen: set[str] = set()\\n 245→    for index, raw_entry in enumerate(raw_entries):\\n 246→        if not isinstance(raw_entry, dict):\\n 247→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry entry #{index} 必须是对象\\\")\\n 248→        _require_keys(\\n 249→ entry_id = raw_entry[\\\"entry_id\\\"]\\n 254→        if not isinstance(entry_id, str) or not entry_id or entry_id in seen:\\n 255→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry entry_id 无效或重复：{entry_id!r}\\\")\\n 256→        if raw_entry[\\\"kind\\\"] not in ENTRY_KINDS:\\n 257→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry kind 未登记：{raw_entry['kind']!r}\\\")\\n 258→        if raw_entry[\\\"action\\\"] not in ACTIONS:\\n 259→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry action 未登记：{raw_entry['action']!r}\\\")\\n 260→        if raw_entry[\\\"hash_mode\\\"] not in HASH_MODES:\\n 261→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry hash_mode 未登记：{raw_entry['hash_mode']!r}\\\")\\n 262→        path = _absolute_clean_path(raw_entry[\\\"path\\\"], f\\\"{entry_id} 267→    paths = [entry[\\\"path\\\"] for entry in entries]\\n 268→    if len(paths) != len(set(paths)):\\n 269→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry path 不得重复\\\")\\n 270→    for parent in entries:\\n 271→        if parent[\\\"action\\\"] != \\\"delete\\\":\\n 272→            continue\\n 273→        parent_path = Path(parent[\\\"path\\\"])\\n 274→        for child in entries:\\n 275→ Path(child[\\\"path\\\"]).relative_to(parent_path)\\n 279→            except ValueError:\\n 280→                continue\\n 281→            raise CutoverError(\\n 282→                f\\\"不得删除父路径同时保留子项：{parent['entry_id']} -> {child['entry_id']}\\\"\\n 283→            )\\n 284→    return roots, sorted(entries, key=lambda item: item[\\\"entry_id\\\"])\\n 285→\\n 286→\\n 287→def build_inventory(registry: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 288→    roots, entries = _parse_registry(registry)\\n 289→    observed = [_observe_entry(entry, roots) for entry in entries]\\n 290→ return payload\\n 304→\\n 305→\\n 306→def registry_for_legacy_root(root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 307→    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build the exact, non-symlink-following registry used by the frozen CLI.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 308→\\n 309→    root = _absolute_clean_path(str(root), \\\"legacy_root\\\")\\n 310→    if not root.exists() or root.is_symlink() or not root.is_dir():\\n 311→        raise 327→\\n 328→    add(root, \\\"legacy_root\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 329→\\n 330→    def visit(directory: Path) -> None:\\n 331→        with os.scandir(directory) as stream:\\n 332→            children \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 340→                visit(path)\\n 341→            else:\\n 342→                # The shorthand must be safe for credential/session carriers whose\\n 343→                # names Exact content hashing is opt-in via\\n 344→                # an explicit reviewed registry.\\n 345→                add(path, \\\"consumer_reference\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 346→\\n 347→ 353→\\n 354→\\n 355→def _verify_inventory(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n 356→    _require_keys(\\n 357→        payload,\\n 358→        {\\n 359→            \\\"schema_version\\\",\\n 360→            \\\"inventory_kind\\\",\\n 361→            \\\"registry_sha256\\\",\\n 362→            \\\"approved_roots\\\",\\n 363→            \\\"entries\\\",\\n 364→            \\\"inventory_sha256\\\",\\n )\\n 536→    if actual != expected:\\n 537→        raise CutoverError(\\\"validation receipt 未绑定当前授权链\\\")\\n 538→\\n 539→\\n 540→def _matches_original(current: dict[str, Any], original: dict[str, Any]]]:\\n 231→    _require_keys(payload, {\\\"schema_version\\\", \\\"approved_roots\\\", \\\"entries\\\"}, \\\"registry\\\")\\n 232→    if payload[\\\"schema_version\\\"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:\\n 233→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry schema_version 必须为 1.0\\\")\\n 234→    raw_roots = payload[\\\"approved_roots\\\"]\\n 235→    raw_entries = payload[\\\"entries\\\"]\\n 236→    if not isinstance(raw_roots, list) or not raw_roots:\\n 237→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry approved_roots 必须是非空列表\\\")\\n 238→    roots = tuple(_absolute_clean_path(value, \\\"approved_root\\\") for value in raw_roots)\\n 239→    if len(set(roots)) != len(roots):\\n 240→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry approved_roots 不得重复\\\")\\n 241→    if not isinstance(raw_entries, list) or not raw_entries:\\n 242→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry entries 必须是非空列表\\\")\\n 243→    entries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\\n 244→    seen: set[str] = set()\\n 245→    for index, raw_entry in enumerate(raw_entries):\\n 246→        if not isinstance(raw_entry, dict):\\n 247→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry entry #{index} 必须是对象\\\")\\n 248→        _require_keys(\\n 249→            raw_entry,\\n 250→            {\\\"entry_id\\\", \\\"kind\\\", \\\"path\\\", \\\"action\\\", \\\"hash_mode\\\"},\\n 251→            f\\\"registry entry #{index}\\\",\\n 252→        )\\n 253→        entry_id = raw_entry[\\\"entry_id\\\"]\\n 254→        if not isinstance(entry_id, str) or not entry_id or entry_id in seen:\\n 255→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry entry_id 无效或重复：{entry_id!r}\\\")\\n 256→        if raw_entry[\\\"kind\\\"] not in ENTRY_KINDS:\\n 257→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry kind 未登记：{raw_entry['kind']!r}\\\")\\n 258→        if raw_entry[\\\"action\\\"] not in ACTIONS:\\n 259→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry action 未登记：{raw_entry['action']!r}\\\")\\n 260→        if raw_entry[\\\"hash_mode\\\"] not in HASH_MODES:\\n 261→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"registry hash_mode 未登记：{raw_entry['hash_mode']!r}\\\")\\n 262→        path = _absolute_clean_path(raw_entry[\\\"path\\\"], f\\\"{entry_id} path\\\")\\n 263→        root = _containing_root(path, roots)\\n 264→        _reject_symlink_ancestors(path, root)\\n 265→        seen.add(entry_id)\\n 266→        entries.append(dict(raw_entry))\\n 267→    paths = [entry[\\\"path\\\"] for entry in entries]\\n 268→    if len(paths) != len(set(paths)):\\n 269→        raise CutoverError(\\\"registry path 不得重复\\\")\\n 270→    for parent in entries:\\n 271→        if parent[\\\"action\\\"] != \\\"delete\\\":\\n 272→            continue\\n 273→        parent_path = Path(parent[\\\"path\\\"])\\n 274→        for child in entries:\\n 275→            if child[\\\"action\\\"] != \\\"retain\\\" or child is parent:\\n 276→                continue\\n 277→            try:\\n 278→                Path(child[\\\"path\\\"]).relative_to(parent_path)\\n 279→            except ValueError:\\n 280→                continue\\n 281→            raise CutoverError(\\n 282→                f\\\"不得删除父路径同时保留子项：{parent['entry_id']} -> {child['entry_id']}\\\"\\n 283→            )\\n 284→    return roots, sorted(entries, key=lambda item: item[\\\"entry_id\\\"])\\n 285→\\n 286→\\n 287→def build_inventory(registry: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 288→    roots, entries = _parse_registry(registry)\\n 289→    observed = [_observe_entry(entry, roots) for entry in entries]\\n 290→    if not any(entry[\\\"exists\\\"] for entry in observed):\\n 291→        raise CutoverError(\\\"inventory 至少必须观察到一个存在的登记项\\\")\\n 292→    for entry in observed:\\n 293→        if (entry[\\\"kind\\\"] == \\\"symlink\\\") != (entry[\\\"node_type\\\"] == \\\"symlink\\\"):\\n 294→            raise CutoverError(f\\\"登记 kind 与符号链接观测不一致：{entry['entry_id']}\\\")\\n 295→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {\\n 296→        \\\"schema_version\\\": SCHEMA_VERSION,\\n 297→        \\\"inventory_kind\\\": \\\"exact-legacy-cutover-v1\\\",\\n 298→        \\\"registry_sha256\\\": canonical_digest(registry),\\n 299→        \\\"approved_roots\\\": [str(root) for root in roots],\\n 300→        \\\"entries\\\": observed,\\n 301→    }\\n 302→    payload[\\\"inventory_sha256\\\"] = canonical_digest(payload)\\n 303→    return payload\\n 304→\\n 305→\\n 306→def registry_for_legacy_root(root: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 307→    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build the exact, non-symlink-following registry used by the frozen CLI.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n 308→\\n 309→    root = _absolute_clean_path(str(root), \\\"legacy_root\\\")\\n 310→    if not root.exists() or root.is_symlink() or not root.is_dir():\\n 311→        raise CutoverError(\\\"legacy_root 必须是存在的普通目录且不得是符号链接\\\")\\n 312→    entries: list[dict[str, str]] = []\\n 313→\\n 314→    def add(path: Path, kind: str, hash_mode: str) -> None:\\n 315→        relative = path.relative_to(root)\\n 316→        suffix = \\\"root\\\" if not relative.parts else relative.as_posix()\\n 317→        entry_id = \\\"legacy-\\\" + hashlib.sha256(suffix.encode()).hexdigest()[:20]\\n 318→        entries.append(\\n 319→            {\\n 320→                \\\"entry_id\\\": entry_id,\\n 321→                \\\"kind\\\": kind,\\n 322→                \\\"path\\\": str(path),\\n 323→                \\\"action\\\": \\\"delete\\\",\\n 324→                \\\"hash_mode\\\": hash_mode,\\n 325→            }\\n 326→        )\\n 327→\\n 328→    add(root, \\\"legacy_root\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 329→\\n 330→    def visit(directory: Path) -> None:\\n 331→        with os.scandir(directory) as stream:\\n 332→            children = sorted(stream, key=lambda item: item.name)\\n 333→        for child in children:\\n 334→            path = directory / child.name\\n 335→            mode = path.lstat().st_mode\\n 336→            if stat.S_ISLNK(mode):\\n 337→                add(path, \\\"symlink\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 338→            elif stat.S_ISDIR(mode):\\n 339→                add(path, \\\"archive_copy\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 340→                visit(path)\\n 341→            else:\\n 342→                # The shorthand must be safe for credential/session carriers whose\\n 343→                # names are not predictable. Exact content hashing is opt-in via\\n 344→                # an explicit reviewed registry.\\n 345→                add(path, \\\"consumer_reference\\\", \\\"metadata\\\")\\n 346→\\n 347→    visit(root)\\n 348→    return {\\n 349→        \\\"schema_version\\\": SCHEMA_VERSION,\\n 350→        \\\"approved_roots\\\": [str(root)],\\n 351→        \\\"entries\\\": entries,\\n 352→    }\\n 353→\\n 354→\\n 355→def _verify_inventory(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n 356→    _require_keys(\\n 357→        payload,\\n 358→        {\\n 359→            \\\"schema_version\\\",\\n 360→            \\\"inventory_kind\\\",\\n 361→            \\\"registry_sha256\\\",\\n 362→            \\\"approved_roots\\\",\\n 363→            \\\"entries\\\",\\n 364→            \\\"inventory_sha256\\\",\\n 365→        },\\n 366→        \\\"inventory\\\",\\n 367→    )\\n 368→    digest = payload[\\\"inventory_sha256\\\"]\\n 369→    body = {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key != \\\"inventory_sha256\\\"}\\n 370→    if digest != canonical_digest(body):\\n 371→        raise CutoverError(\\\"inventory_sha256 不匹配\\\")\\n 372→\\n 373→\\n 374→def authorization_digest(payload: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\\n 375→    body = {key: value for key, value in payload.items() if key != \\\"authorization_sha256\\\"}\\n 376→    return canonical_digest(body)\\n 377→\\n 378→\\n 379→def _verify_authorization(payload: dict[str, Any], inventory: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n 380→    _require_keys(\\n 381→        payload,\\n 382→        {\\n 383→            \\\"schema_version\\\",\\n 384→            \\\"authorization_kind\\\",\\n 385→            \\\"inventory_sha256\\\",\\n 386→            \\\"registry_sha256\\\",\\n 387→            \\\"recovery_receipt_sha256\\\",\\n 388→ return canonical_digest(payload)\\n 413→\\n 414→\\n 415→def _verify_recovery(recovery: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n 416→    _require_keys(\\n 417→        recovery,\\n 418→        {\\n 419→            \\\"schema_version\\\",\\n 420→            \\\"receipt_kind\\\",\\n 421→            \\\"status\\\",\\n 422→            \\\"recovery_package_sha256\\\",\\n 423→            \\\"issued_at\\\",\\n 424→        },\\n 425→        \\\"recovery receipt\\\",\\n 426→    )\\n 427→    if recovery[\\\"schema_version\\\"] != SCHEMA_VERSION:\\n 428→        raise CutoverError(\\\"恢复包回执 schema_version 必须为 validate_cutover(\\n 448→    inventory: dict[str, Any], authorization: dict[str, Any], recovery: dict[str, Any]\\n 449→) -> dict[str, Any]:\\n 450→    _verify_inventory(inventory)\\n 451→ \\\"receipt_kind\\\": \\\"legacy-cutover-validation-v1\\\",\\n 465→        \\\"status\\\": \\\"validated\\\",\\n 466→        \\\"inventory_sha256\\\": inventory[\\\"inventory_sha256\\\"],\\n 467→ receipt[\\\"validation_sha256\\\"] = canonical_digest(receipt)\\n 500→    return receipt\\n 501→\\n 502→\\n 503→def _verify_validation(\\n 504→    receipt: dict[str, Any], inventory: dict[str, Any], \\\"receipt_kind\\\",\\n 511→            \\\"status\\\",\\n 512→            \\\"inventory_sha256\\\",\\n 513→            \\\"authorization_sha256\\\",\\n 514→            \\\"recovery_receipt_sha256\\\",\\n 515→            \\\"validation_sha256\\\",\\n 516→        },\\n 517→        \\\"validation receipt\\\",\\n 518→    )\\n 519→    body = {key: value for key, value in )\\n 536→    if actual != expected:\\n 537→        raise CutoverError(\\\"validation receipt 未绑定当前授权链\\\")\\n 538→\\n 539→\\n 540→def _matches_original(current: dict[str, Any], original: dict[str, 628→    receipt[\\\"apply_sha256\\\"] = canonical_digest(receipt)\\n 629→    return receipt\\n 630→\\n 631→\\n 632→def absence_check(\\n 633→    inventory: dict[str, Any], scan_roots: tuple[Path, ...] And `current` is computed from inventory[\\\"entries\\\"] in the same order. OK.\\n\\n**Observation H — potential TOCTOU in apply:** Between `_observe_entry` (lstat) and `path.unlink()`, an attacker could replace the path with a symlink to something else. `path.unlink()` on a symlink unlinks the symlink itself, not the target — safe. `path.rmdir()` on a directory: if a directory was replaced by a symlink to a directory outside, `rmdir()` would fail with ENOTDIR (symlink is not a dir for rmdir). Actually rmdir on a symlink raises ENOTDIR. Safe. If a directory is replaced by another directory containing content — rmdir fails unless empty. So the post-check `path.exists() or path.is_symlink()` catches persistence. There's a subtle race: between unlink and the exists() check, a new file could appear — but it'd be created by someone with write access to the parent; the tool reports failure then. Acceptable residual (documented as visibility not signature).\\n\\n**Observation I — `_reject_symlink_ancestors` partial check:** it checks ancestors from root down to parent, but if a *missing* intermediate ancestor is encountered (`FileNotFoundError` → return), it stops checking deeper ancestors — wait no, it returns, meaning deeper ancestors are not checked. If the path doesn't exist (parent missing), that's fine because the target itself doesn't exist (will be observed absent). But if the target path's parent chain doesn't exist but target was created later... inventory already captured. Fine.\\n\\nHowever, note: `_reject_symlink_ancestors` only checks ancestors *within* the approved root (relative parts). The root itself must not be a symlink. But what about ancestors ABOVE the root (e.g., /tmp itself being a symlink)? `realpath` is recorded, and `_containing_root` compares lexical paths. If the root is /tmp/x and /tmp is a symlink to /private/tmp (macOS!), then the lexical containment and lstat-based checks are on the lexical path — lstat on /tmp/x follows the /tmp symlink at the OS level. On macOS, /tmp IS a symlink to /private/tmp. But the test fixtures use tmp_path (pytest) which resolves to /private/var/folders/... — not symlinked. For real usage on macOS, the legacy root would be under /Users/... which has no symlink ancestors. This is a known limitation: lexical path containment doesn't detect symlinked ancestors above the root. Since realpath is recorded and compared across inventory/validate/apply, drift would be caught if the realpath changed. But realpath computed once per observe — the comparison `current != inventory[\\\"entries\\\"]` compares realpath strings; if /tmp/x resolves differently at different times, drift caught. Acceptable.\\n\\n**Observation J — the \\\"metadata\\\" hash_mode for directories in `registry_for_legacy_root`:** dirs registered as \\\"archive_copy\\\" with hash_mode metadata → `_metadata_digest` on a directory gives mode/size/type — size of a directory is not stable across OS/filesystem (dir size can change without content change on some FS). On APFS, directory st_size is typically 96 or 192 bytes and may vary. This means a metadata digest of a directory could drift spuriously between inventory and validate, causing a false failure — fail-closed direction, not dangerous, but could block apply. It's fail-closed, so acceptable, though worth noting.\\n\\nActually wait — for `registry_for_legacy_root`, dirs are `archive_copy` with metadata hash_mode. In `validate`, the digest must match. Directory st_size on APFS: typically stable at 96 or changes when entries added/removed. Since apply removes children first (entry by entry) and then the directory — but validate happens BEFORE apply. Between inventory and apply, if the directory's size changed due to external writes, drift is detected (correct behavior — drift means something changed). The `_matches_directory_after_child_deletes` handles the resumable case by ignoring sha256 for directories. OK.\\n\\n**Observation K — CRITICAL issue in `apply_cutover` resumable semantics:** `_matches_directory_after_child_deletes` ignores sha256 for directories when resuming. That means: after a partial apply that deleted some children, a directory entry can pass the pre-check with `exists=True` and unchanged metadata. But `_matches_directory_after_child_deletes` keys do not include sha256 — it compares entry_id/kind/path/approved_root/action/hash_mode/exists/realpath/device/inode/node_type. Device+inode must match. Fine.\\n\\nBut here's a subtle bug: in the pre-apply check loop:\\n```python\\nfor observed, original in zip(current, inventory[\\\"entries\\\"], strict=True):\\n    if original[\\\"action\\\"] == \\\"delete\\\" and not observed[\\\"exists\\\"]:\\n        continue\\n```\\nFor entries with action=retain and exists=False — the check `unchanged` will fail because observed has exists=False but original has exists=True — raising drift. That's fine (retain on absent path is drift).\\n\\n**Observation L — `absence_check` scan_roots:** checks that each inventory entry path is contained in the provided scan roots. If scan_roots are given, entries must be under them. It doesn't scan for unregistered references (out of scope per PRD — absence is over the registered inventory only). Good.\\n\\n**Observation N — `inventory` requires at least one existing entry.** `if not any(entry[\\\"exists\\\"] ...): raise`. For a fully-absent legacy root (already migrated), build_inventory fails. This means absence_check can't be run with a fresh inventory after everything is gone — but absence_check is run with the original inventory (from before apply). Fine. That's a potential gap: the doc claims apply requires prior validation, but the code permits skipping it (validation=None). So apply still checks drift against the inventory. The validation receipt adds a formal \\\"validated\\\" step but apply re-does the same checks. So the gap is that the doc's claim (\\\"只接受已经验证的同一授权链\\\") is stronger than the code (\\\"授权链匹配且当前状态无漂移\\\"). It's fail-closed anyway. Minor doc/code inconsistency — worth flagging as P2/nit.\\n\\nHmm wait, actually let me reconsider: is validation required by the design? design.md: \\\"apply：仅处理 delete 或 retain 等已登记动作；要求授权摘要精确匹配已验证清单；拒绝 symlink-follow 和根逃逸；重复执行返回相同终态。\\\" — \\\"已验证清单\\\" = validated inventory; the apply itself re-verifies inventory digest + current-state match. OK — doc wording slightly stronger than code; P3.\\n\\n**Defect candidate 8 — REAL ISSUE: `inventory` requires ≥1 existing entry (build_inventory raises \\\"至少必须观察到一个存在的登记项\\\" if all absent).** Consider Task 10.8 legacy-absence closure: after apply, to re-verify absence you need an inventory — but you keep the ORIGINAL inventory (pre-apply), which still records entries; absence_check works because the inventory file persists. But for an entry whose containing root is B (deeper), ancestors BETWEEN A and B are not checked — they're above the chosen root. If A→B path contains a symlink above B (e.g., /x/y where /x itself... no wait, /x is root A itself; if /x is a symlink, only checked when root is /x). Actually it iterates `current = root; for part in ancestors: current /= part` — starting at root, so /x/y's ancestors *within the root* are checked, not /x. But the lexical path /x/y/file traverses /x at OS level. If /x is a symlink, the lstat-based checks on /x/y/file all happen through the symlink — realpath would differ from the lexical path, and drift between inventory and validate would only catch realpath drift if... realpath IS recorded, and inventory/validate both compute it the same way — so a symlinked /x gives consistent (wrong) realpaths, no drift detected. The approved root itself must not be a symlink, but the *parent chain above the approved root* is NOT checked. For multi-root registries where a deep root is nested under another approved root that's a symlink... this is exotic. The primary risk: approved root /tmp/legacy where /tmp is a symlink (macOS). Then the tool would operate on the real directory through the symlink — the lexical path is under the approved root, so no escape; realpath recorded would be /private/tmp/legacy. Consistent behavior, no escape. Acceptable — lexical containment is about the *declared* path; the OS resolves parent symlinks uniformly. Not a defect for the stated threat model (the threat is entries escaping the approved root, not the root itself being reached via a symlinked parent — though a reviewer might argue an approved root reached through an attacker-controlled symlink parent could be swapped between inventory and apply, changing which directory is acted on. Since realpath is recorded and compared, a swap changing the resolution target would change realpath → drift caught. Actually wait: is realpath recomputed at apply time? Current `_parser()` matches; `_success_signal` output now matches the test's asserted strings (`CUTOVER_INVENTORY_OK mutated=0`, `CUTOVER_VALIDATE_OK escapes=0 credentials=0 recovery=passed`, `CUTOVER_APPLY_OK`, `LEGACY_ABSENCE_OK`). **P2: the success signal hardcodes `escapes=0 credentials=0` without computing either.** Escapes are true-by-construction (any escape raises before success). But \\\"credentials=0\\\" is a security claim with no detector behind it: vacuously true under `--legacy-root` (metadata-only, nothing read), and **false in general** under an explicit content-mode registry where `_directory_digest`/`file_digest` hash file bytes that may include credential-like files. An automation consumer grepping `credentials=0` would be misled. Fix now (before Task 10.7 freezes on it): make the signal mode-aware, drop the credential claim, or reword to `content_read=no|yes`.\\n\\n**(c) Multi-root registry — PASS with nuance (P3).** `approved_roots` is a list; `_containing_root` picks the longest lexical match; duplicate roots rejected; per-entry root containment enforced; registry digest binds the whole multi-root payload. Nuance: `_reject_symlink_ancestors` checks the approved root and ancestors *within* it, but not symlinks *above* the root (e.g., macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`; or a segment between two overlapping approved roots). This is mitigated because `realpath` is recorded at inventory and recomputed at validate/apply, so a retargeted parent symlink changes `realpath` and trips the drift comparison (fail-closed). Recommend documenting this boundary rather than changing code.\\n\\n**(d) No sensitive-content reads — PASS under the contract's two modes.** `--legacy-root` shorthand: all entries `hash_mode: metadata` (pinned by `test_legacy_root_shortcut_never_reads_unknown_file_content`); metadata digest = mode/size/type (+link text for symlinks) — no content bytes. Explicit registry: `content` mode is opt-in and requires human review of the registry JSON (documented in code comment and doc); `_directory_digest` under content mode recursively reads **all** nested file contents — this is the one place sensitive content could be read, and it is gated only by reviewer discipline. This matches PRD (\\\"Exact content hashing is opt-in via an explicit reviewed registry\\\") — accepted, but I flag it as the single sharpest corner of the \\\"never read\\\" claim.\\n\\n**(e) Fail-closed semantics — PASS, verified line-by-line.**\\n- Path escape: `_absolute_clean_path` (absolute, no `..`) + `_containing_root` containment; tested.\\n- Symlink: root itself must not be a symlink (`_reject_symlink_ancestors` lines 113-118, added since the execution audit); entry ancestors checked; leaf symlinks only read-link-target + unlinked, never followed; kind-vs-observation consistency check in `build_inventory`; tested (alias-root, kind drift).\\n- Inode/device/type/digest drift: `validate_cutover`/`validate_inventory`/`apply_cutover` re-observe and compare the full entry dict including `device`/`inode`/`realpath`/`sha256`; tested (content mutation → 漂移).\\n- Authorization: self-digest + `inventory_sha256` + `registry_sha256` + exact `actions` list equality; tested.\\n- Recovery: consumer contract `_verify_recovery` (kind `recovery-package-v1`, status `passed`, lowercase 64-hex sha256, tz-aware ISO8601; unknown fields rejected); gate failures tested via parametrized `test_recovery_receipt_gate_fails_closed`.\\n- Idempotence: `apply_cutover` second run reports `already_absent`/`retained`; tested. Verdict\n\n**P0 = 0, P1 = 0 on the current bytes.** Three P2s and several P3s follow; none blocks acceptance, but two should be resolved before Task 10.7 freezes the CLI contract, and one requires a Codex semantic decision. All six challenge areas of the objective hold under static analysis, with the caveats below. **Runtime verification (pytest/ruff/mypy on current bytes) could not be performed by me (Bash denied) and remains Codex's check.**\n\n### 3. Per-challenge-area findings\n\n**(a) Real legacy root zero-touch — PASS.** No default path anywhere in `tools/legacy_cutover.py` (verified: `--legacy-root`/`--registry` are required, mutually exclusive; `apply`/`validate`/`absence-check` take only explicit files). `registry_for_legacy_root` requires an explicit existing non-symlink dir. All 10 tests in `tests/migration/test_legacy_cutover.py` build fixtures exclusively under `tmp_path` (verified line-by-line: `_fixture`, `_registry`, `test_partial_apply...` etc.); the CLI test passes only `tmp_path` arguments. `tests/migration/test_no_legacy_runtime_dependency.py` also uses `tmp_path`. No hardcoded user paths in the tool. The only path that could ever touch a real root is Task 10.7's own deliberate invocation — out of Task 10.5 scope. Task 10.4 checkpoint confirms the real root was unmodified and unread-executed.\n\n**(b) Frozen CLI compatibility — PASS with one overclaim (P2).** `test_cli_matches_frozen_task_107_and_108_argument_contract` (lines 230-318) pins: `inventory --legacy-root|--registry --manifest/--output`, `validate --manifest --authorization? --legacy-root? --recovery-receipt --output?`, `apply --manifest --authorization --validation-receipt? --receipt/--output`, `absence-check --manifest --scan-root? --output?`. Current `_parser()` matches; `_success_signal` output now matches the test's asserted strings (`CUTOVER_INVENTORY_OK mutated=0`, `CUTOVER_VALIDATE_OK escapes=0 credentials=0 recovery=passed`, `CUTOVER_APPLY_OK`, `LEGACY_ABSENCE_OK`). **P2: the success signal hardcodes `escapes=0 credentials=0` without computing either.** Escapes are true-by-construction (any escape raises before success). But \"credentials=0\" is a security claim with no detector behind it: vacuously true under `--legacy-root` (metadata-only, nothing read), and **false in general** under an explicit content-mode registry where `_directory_digest`/`file_digest` hash file bytes that may include credential-like files. An automation consumer grepping `credentials=0` would be misled. Fix now (before Task 10.7 freezes on it): make the signal mode-aware, drop the credential claim, or reword to `content_read=no|yes`.\n\n**(c) Multi-root registry — PASS with nuance (P3).** `approved_roots` is a list; `_containing_root` picks the longest lexical match; duplicate roots rejected; per-entry root containment enforced; registry digest binds the whole multi-root payload. Nuance: `_reject_symlink_ancestors` checks the approved root and ancestors *within* it, but not symlinks *above* the root (e.g., macOS `/tmp -> /private/tmp`; or a segment between two overlapping approved roots). This is mitigated because `realpath` is recorded at inventory and recomputed at validate/apply, so a retargeted parent symlink changes `realpath` and trips the drift comparison (fail-closed). Recommend documenting this boundary rather than changing code.\n\n**(d) No sensitive-content reads — PASS under the contract's two modes.** `--legacy-root` shorthand: all entries `hash_mode: metadata` (pinned by `test_legacy_root_shortcut_never_reads_unknown_file_content`); metadata digest = mode/size/type (+link text for symlinks) — no content bytes. Explicit registry: `content` mode is opt-in and requires human review of the registry JSON (documented in code comment and doc); `_directory_digest` under content mode recursively reads **all** nested file contents — this is the one place sensitive content could be read, and it is gated only by reviewer discipline. This matches PRD (\"Exact content hashing is opt-in via an explicit reviewed registry\") — accepted, but I flag it as the single sharpest corner of the \"never read\" claim.\n\n**(e) Fail-closed semantics — PASS, verified line-by-line.**\n- Path escape: `_absolute_clean_path` (absolute, no `..`) + `_containing_root` containment; tested.\n- Symlink: root itself must not be a symlink (`_reject_symlink_ancestors` lines 113-118, added since the execution audit); entry ancestors checked; leaf symlinks only read-link-target + unlinked, never followed; kind-vs-observation consistency check in `build_inventory`; tested (alias-root, kind drift).\n- Inode/device/type/digest drift: `validate_cutover`/`validate_inventory`/`apply_cutover` re-observe and compare the full entry dict including `device`/`inode`/`realpath`/`sha256`; tested (content mutation → 漂移).\n- Authorization: self-digest + `inventory_sha256` + `registry_sha256` + exact `actions` list equality; tested.\n- Recovery: consumer contract `_verify_recovery` (kind `recovery-package-v1`, status `passed`, lowercase 64-hex sha256, tz-aware ISO8601; unknown fields rejected); gate failures tested via parametrized `test_recovery_receipt_gate_fails_closed`.\n- Idempotence: `apply_cutover` second run reports `already_absent`/`retained`; tested. Partial-failure resume: children-first ordering by path depth; `_matches_directory_after_child_deletes` (sha256-exempt for directories) enables resumption with the same authorization; tested (`test_partial_apply_is_fail_closed_and_same_authorization_resumes`).\n- Residue: `absence_check` fails on any still-existing registered entry **including `retain`** (pinned by `test_absence_check_does_not_treat_retain_as_absent`); final gate must use a full-disposition registry (documented in design.md) — this resolves the earlier retain-absence \"deadlock\" concern by contract, not by code.\n- Structural contradictions: delete-parent+retain-child rejected; duplicate entry_id/path rejected; all-empty inventory rejected.\n- TOCTOU residue: between `lstat` and `unlink`/`rmdir`, a swap is bounded — `unlink` on a symlink removes the link (never the target), `rmdir` on a symlink fails `ENOTDIR`, and the post-delete `exists()/is_symlink()` check re-arms; the doc honestly states digests are \"漂移可见性，不是数字签名\".\n\n**(f) Pre-RC vs release-receipt layering and pending/not_applicable semantics — PASS with one open semantic decision (P2).**\n- Layering: catalog `receipt.schema` must equal `schemas/host-receipt.schema.json` (pre-RC host envelope, pinned per case); the final owner-stage envelope is `release-case-receipt.schema.json`, verified with `additionalProperties: false`, status-conditional `allOf`, tz-aware `issued_at`, self-`receipt_digest`.\n- Bindings verified: case_id, release_scope, owner_task, catalog_sha256 (file bytes), case_digest (recomputed via `compute_case_digest` with `case_digest` excluded, reports/formats sorted, inputs path-sorted), package_manifest_sha256, release_candidate_sha256, input_sha256 (recomputed from sorted inputs), plus schema-required run_id/session_id/artifact_sha256/verdict_sha256/project_contract_sha256/not_applicable_reason_zh/issued_at.\n- `pending_future_owner` never closes: any status ≠ `accepted` (except the restricted `not_applicable`) raises `未在 owner 阶段 accepted`; tested.\n- `not_applicable` restricted: requires catalog `execution_scope == conditional-extension` **and** a `not_applicable` string, binds `project_contract_sha256` to the actual contract file and reason equality to the catalog string; catalog-side rules additionally pinned by `test_fixture_catalog.py::_assert_required_owner_and_state_rules` (only `optional-adapter-recovery` may be conditional-extension/not_applicable; E1 reason must mention 合同摘要). Tested with five mutation cases.\n- Path hardening: `_safe_receipt_path` rejects symlink root/receipt/ancestors and enforces resolution inside the receipts root; tested.\n\n### 4. Findings register\n\n| ID | Sev | Finding | Recommendation |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| F1 | P2 | `apply_cutover` accepts `validation=None` and CLI `apply` accepts no `--validation-receipt`, while `docs/acceptance/legacy-cutover-tooling.md` states \"apply 只接受已经验证的同一授权链\". Security impact is nil (apply re-verifies inventory digest, authorization chain, and live FS drift itself), but the doc contract is stronger than enforcement — a Task 10.8 automation could skip the formal validation step. | Freeze now: make `--validation-receipt` required in the CLI contract (and `validation` required in `apply_cutover`), or reword the doc to \"授权链匹配 + 当前状态无漂移\". |\n| F2 | P2 | `CUTOVER_VALIDATE_OK ... credentials=0` is asserted without any credential-detection logic; false for content-mode registries. | Make the signal mode-aware or drop the credential claim (see challenge (b)). |\n| F3 | P2 | Aggregator never cross-checks catalog `receipt.owner_status` (`pending_future_owner`) against receipt `status`; a case still declared pending in the catalog can be closed by an `accepted` release receipt. Catalog-side semantics are pinned by `test_fixture_catalog.py`, but the aggregator is decoupled from them. Possibly intentional (catalog stays pending; the release receipt is the RC-time artifact) — needs Codex's ruling. | Either enforce \"accepted requires catalog owner_status ∈ {current_owner, verified}\" (if the catalog is expected to transition at freeze), or document explicitly that the decoupling is intentional and that closure authority is the governed receipts-root + freeze record, not the catalog field. |\n| F4 | P3 | `--scan-root` on `absence-check` is a containment sanity gate only; it never scans for unregistered remnants (registered-only is per PRD). Flag name over-promises. | Document as sanity gate; optionally rename. |\n| F5 | P3 | Recovery-package receipt has a consumer contract only (`_verify_recovery`); no schema file and no producer exists in Task 10.5. | Task 10.6 must define the producer contract with at least `receipt_kind/status/recovery_package_sha256/issued_at`; record in 10.5 checkpoint as cross-task dependency. |\n| F6 | P3 | Catalog `receipt.relative_path` (e.g. `docs/acceptance/receipts/required-v12/<case>.json`) is never consumed; aggregator locates receipts as `<case_id>.json` flat under `receipts_root`. Works in practice only if the runner points `receipts_root` at the case-id directory level. | Document the flat `<case_id>.json` contract in the acceptance note, or honor `relative_path`. |\n| F7 | P3 | `validate_inventory` accepts `legacy_root=None` (CLI `validate` without `--authorization` and without `--legacy-root` issues a receipt with no root binding). Fail-closed downstream (apply re-checks), but the receipt over-promises. | Require either `--authorization` or `--legacy-root` in the CLI contract. |\n| F8 | P3 | `_safe_receipt_path` propagates `FileNotFoundError` (not `ReceiptClosureError`) when `receipts_root` is missing; caught in CLI `main` but inconsistent in library use. | Wrap in `ReceiptClosureError`. |\n| F9 | P3 | `tools/legacy_cutover.py` changed during my pass (734→750 lines; `_success_signal` added). The recorded \"24 focused + 162 regressions passed\" predates or coincides with this byte change — unverifiable by me. | Codex: confirm whether a bounded repair landed in-window and re-run the suites on current bytes; record it in the checkpoint. |\n| F10 | P3 | `_directory_digest` under `content` mode reads the full nested tree (O(tree), includes any sensitive bytes); the two new tools and `legacy-cutover-tooling.md` are not declared in `package-manifest.json` nor in the bundle allowlist (consistent: they are governance tools, not runtime components; `release-case-receipt.schema.json` **is** declared, so the manifest schema-closure test holds). | No change needed; note in checkpoint that tools are intentionally repo-local. |\n\n### 5. Highest-impact objections (ranked)\n\n1. **F2 — a false security claim in the frozen CLI success signal.** This is the item most likely to be consumed blindly by Task 10.7/10.8 automation. Cheap to fix now.\n2. **F1 — doc-vs-code contract looseness on the validation receipt.** If left, Task 10.8 ships a weaker gate than the approval note promises.\n3. **F3 — owner-stage semantics ambiguity.** Not a defect under the \"visibility, not signature\" doctrine, but the closure's authority ultimately rests on the governed process that writes `receipts_root`; the aggregator only detects drift. Codex should record the receipts-root path + freeze run evidence explicitly, or a future reviewer will ask exactly this question.\n4. **F9 — evidence-integrity during the conference window.** My audit is of bytes that changed mid-pass;
